# The explicit source lists of the isolated test executables: every production translation unit such an
# executable compiles, every first-party header those units reach, every stub standing in for a production
# unit and why, every include directory, and every macro it is compiled with.
#
# The plan's section 8.1 names this file for the migration test executables: a migrated source closure is proved
# in a test executable of its own before its library is whole, and that proof is worth only what the executable
# actually compiles. The consumer suites' old factory compiled a suite's whole directory glob and added
# controls/IAppContext.cpp and app/AfterNightEvent.cpp to every suite whether it used them or not, so a suite
# could pass while its closure silently held files nobody listed. An isolated target compiles this list and
# nothing else: build/premake/afternight-test-pm5.lua's addIsolatedTestProject reads it, and
# build/scripts/tools/consumer_targets.py holds both the list and the generated project to it - every named file
# exists, no entry is a pattern, every stub has a reason, every first-party file the units reach is named, the
# units reach no header of an engine the target's class excludes, and the generated project compiles exactly the
# units and includes exactly the directories named here.
#
# A target compiles a list when its `sources` field in consumer_targets.txt is `@<name>`. The factory adds the engine's
# own include directories for the target's class - none for class none, the engine's published headers for class engine
# - and defines AFTERNIGHT_TESTS, as every suite of the test workspace is compiled; neither is repeated here.
#
# Record format - one per executable, entries indented four spaces:
#
#   sources <target name>
#       unit     <path of a translation unit> [only=linux | only=windows]
#       header   <path of a first-party header a unit reaches>
#       stub     <path of a translation unit under src/AfterNightTests/> <why it stands in for a production unit>
#       include  <an include directory, as a repository path>
#       define   <a macro the executable is compiled with>
#       link     <a registry library the executable links beyond its class's engine - AfterNightPlatform,
#                AfterNightConsumerRuntime, or an optional adapter check_build_graph.py names it a consumer of>
#       external <a third-party library that is no registry target, by the name the isolated factory knows its
#                include directory, macros and link line by - kddockwidgets, python for a suite that starts the bundled
#                interpreter, or tbb for a suite that calls oneTBB directly>
#       qt       <the Qt modules it uses, space-separated - core gui widgets>
#
# Lines starting with '#' and blank lines are ignored.

# The system-information panel's memory parser: its cases, linked against the Controls library, class engine as that
# library is.
sources Test_SystemInformationPanel
    unit     src/AfterNightTests/Test_SystemInformationPanel/TestMain.cpp
    unit     src/AfterNightTests/Test_SystemInformationPanel/Test_SystemMemoryInfoParser_Linux.cpp only=linux
    unit     src/AfterNightTests/Test_SystemInformationPanel/Test_SystemMemoryInfoParser_Windows.cpp only=windows
    header   src/AfterNightControls/AfterNightControlsExport.h
    header   src/AfterNightControls/controls/SystemMemoryInfoParser.h
    include  src/AfterNightControls
    link     AfterNightControls

# The Controls seams, class engine: the one Controls suite that compiles Controls sources rather than linking the
# library, because its cases need the classes they exercise compiled into the executable. The close-during-work cases of
# the view, the histogram and statistics windows and the drawing canvas run under the freed-memory quarantine, which
# replaces the global allocation functions and so holds a class's freed blocks only when the class is compiled here; the
# case that observes what the view tells its companions does it through the stand-ins its companion seam is implemented
# by here. It compiles the statistics windows' and the drawing tools' closures over the viewer's, each stand-in
# replacing a companion whose real unit the list does not compile. Every other Controls case links the library.

# The integration workflow's suite, linked against the processes library: the model, the frame import over the engine's
# probe and the error record an engine failure maps to; the workflow core - manifest, diff, hashing, snapshot, tracker,
# planner, frame cache and executor - with the persistence contracts their records are read and written through and the
# stage companions the library defines; the nine stages; the stores, the report, the archiver and every window of the
# integration and its Lite front end; over frames the neutral fixture layer writes.
